Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Skill abstraction---the process of learning reusable and temporally extended behaviors---has emerged as a key paradigm for improving sample efficiency and generalization in robot learning. For efficient skill transfer to real robots, learned skills must generalize across tasks and domains, remain robust to visual and dynamic perturbations, and be efficient enough for practical deployment. However, existing methods typically satisfy only a subset of these properties, as they capture either high-level semantic intent (what) or low-level motion dynamics (how). This incomplete skill transfer yields weak priors for policy learning, thereby demanding substantial in-domain data for downstream adaptation. To address these challenges, we introduce BooST, a two-stage framework that explicitly bridges semantics and motions to satisfy all three desiderata. BooST first leverages a cross-modal VQ-VAE to capture both semantic intent and motion dynamics, yielding a unified skill representation. It then distills this representation into a lightweight policy for efficient downstream adaptation to new tasks. Extensive experiments across simulation and real-robot settings demonstrate that BooST achieves superior few-shot adaptation, cross-domain skill transfer, and robustness to dynamic visual distractors, while maintaining a lightweight yet expressive design suitable for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [131] [Toward the Cognitive--Physical Limits of Embodied Intelligence through a World-Model-Centric Autonomous Racing Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10618)
*Zitong Shan,Baichuan Lou,Yanxin Zhou,Shuge Wu,Xianqi He,Bolin Zhao,Sheng Zhao,Zhouheng Li,Chee Kiong Ong,King Ho Holden Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种以世界模型为中心的自主赛车智能体，通过闭环优化探索认知与物理极限，在高速赛车场景下实现高成功率交互。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于自动驾驶赛车（autonomous racing）的 embodied AI 世界模型方法，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、边缘/星上部署等轻量化技术。虽然涉及 onboard system 和实时感知，但场景和任务与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：自主赛车（autonomous racing）中的世界模型学习与极限控制。场景：高速赛车赛道，真实车辆测试（最高256.3 km/h）和仿真环境。传感器类型：摘要未说明具体传感器（提及高频定位与感知，但未说明是相机、LiDAR等）。目标类别：无目标检测任务，关注的是赛车交互与动力学极限。

Model architecture: 世界模型为中心的框架（world-model-centric autonomous racing agent），包含世界状态构建（world-state construction）、未来感知推理（future-aware reasoning）、近极限控制（near-limit control），通过闭环优化（closed-loop refinement）迭代更新世界模型和策略。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提及 onboard system 在256.3 km/h下保持鲁棒定位与感知，但未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据：从真实车辆自主赛车中采集（near-limit successes and failures）。指标：交互成功率（interaction success rate）88.3%，最高速度256.3 km/h，峰值侧向加速度26.8 m/s²。未使用遥感领域标准数据集。

Experiments: 在真实赛车中采集数据，在全尺寸仿真中评估，世界模型智能体达到88.3%交互成功率。闭环优化提升了认知-物理极限利用、故障恢复能力和跨场景泛化能力。未与遥感目标检测方法对比。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度、参数量等轻量化关键指标。场景（赛车）与遥感（卫星对地观测）差异巨大，方法不可直接迁移。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可抽象参考：(1) 世界模型（world model）思想——在遥感领域可类比为对地观测场景的动态建模，例如卫星轨道变化下的目标外观变化预测；(2) 闭环优化（closed-loop refinement）——在轨模型可考虑利用星上推理结果反馈更新模型参数或检测策略；(3) 从失败样本中学习（learning from near-limit failures）——在轨检测中可收集误检/漏检样本进行增量学习。但这些均为间接启发，非直接可复现方法。

Abstract: Embodied artificial intelligence aims to develop agents that perceive, reason, and act through continuous interaction with the physical world. However, most embodied systems are still evaluated within conservative safety margins or moderate interaction regimes, leaving their capability boundaries under extreme conditions insufficiently understood. Autonomous racing provides a stringent testbed by combining high-frequency localization and perception, adversarial interaction, near-saturated vehicle dynamics, and strict safety constraints. Existing systems push high-speed performance but rarely model and refine cognitive and physical limits jointly. Here we show that a world-model-centric autonomous racing agent provides a concrete step toward exploring these coupled limits. The framework learns predictive world models from near-limit successes and failures to capture interaction evolution, ego dynamics, and feasible-motion boundaries, coupling world-state construction, future-aware reasoning, and near-limit control in a closed-loop refinement process. Training data were collected from real-vehicle autonomous racing, where the onboard system maintained robust localization and perception at speeds up to 256.3 km/h and peak lateral acceleration of 26.8 m/s$^2$. In full-scale simulated racing, the well trained world-model-centric agent achieves an 88.3% interaction success rate across various challenging simulated racing scenarios. Closed-loop refinement of the world model and policy further improved utilization of cognitive-physical limits, recovery from failure modes, and generalization across varying conditions and unseen circuits. These results suggest a boundary-aware methodology in which world models help embodied agents represent, predict, and continually refine their capability boundaries for safer real-world deployment.

</details>


### [132] [When Your State Estimator Has Lost The Plot: Detecting Estimator Failures Via Spectral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10623)
*Christian Lanegger,Helen Oleynikova,Roland Siegwart,Michael Pantic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于频域功率分析的传感器无关的估计器健康状态评估方法，用于检测机器人状态估计的退化或故障。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于机器人状态估计的可靠性评估（introspective estimation health assessment），不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星在轨部署或模型轻量化。虽然提到了"lightweight introspective tool"，但这里的"轻量"指的是频域分析的计算开销小，而非深度学习模型的轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：状态估计器健康评估（estimator health assessment）；应用场景：空中机器人（aerial robot）的室外飞行；传感器类型：视觉-惯性、LiDAR-惯性、雷达-惯性里程计（visual-inertial, LiDAR-inertial, radar-inertial odometry）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的模型架构。方法基于频域分析（frequency-domain analysis），包括信号功率（signal power）、频谱带宽（spectral bandwidth）、熵（entropy）等指标，不涉及深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 论文中的"lightweight"指频域分析本身计算开销小，可作为轻量级内省工具（lightweight introspective tool），但并非深度学习模型压缩/加速方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。与本研究方向的轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署指标。仅提到该方法计算简单（simple frequency-domain analysis），可作为轻量级内省工具，但未给出具体量化数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：使用空中机器人室外飞行数据（outdoor flight data），包含视觉-惯性、LiDAR-惯性、雷达-惯性里程计的多模态数据，数据集包含多次估计器故障（multiple estimator failures）。评价指标：检测率（detection of 51%-58% of labeled failures）、精确率（60%-84% precision）。

Experiments: 在三种不同的状态估计框架（visual-inertial, LiDAR-inertial, radar-inertial odometry）上评估，观察到健康估计与退化估计之间存在一致的频谱功率差异，可检测51%-58%的标记故障，精确率60%-84%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化完全无关；2. 仅检测51%-58%的故障，召回率较低；3. 仅在飞行机器人数据上验证，未涉及卫星或遥感场景；4. 未讨论深度学习模型压缩或加速方法。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人课题基本无关，无可借鉴点。频域分析作为一种轻量级信号处理方法，理论上可迁移到遥感图像质量评估或传感器故障检测，但与目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。

Abstract: Reliable onboard state estimation is essential for safe robotic operation, yet unmodeled disturbances, such as sensor aliasing or out-of-distribution noise, still cause estimators to degrade or fail completely. While many methods aim to improve estimator robustness, only a few provide introspective mechanisms to assess estimate quality. Existing uncertainty measures, such as covariances, rely on idealized assumptions and tend to be overconfident, and more recent data-driven approaches are typically tied to their training data distributions.
  We propose a sensor-agnostic introspective method that assesses estimator health by analyzing the frequency-domain power distribution of recent velocity estimates. The method is evaluated using outdoor flight data from an aerial robot running visual-inertial, LiDAR-inertial, and radar-inertial odometry. The dataset includes multiple estimator failures, enabling analysis of several frequency-domain indicators, such as signal power, spectral bandwidth, and entropy. We observe consistent spectral power differences between healthy and degraded estimates, allowing detection of 51%-58% of labeled failures with 60%-84% precision across three fundamentally different state estimation frameworks. Our results show that even a simple frequency-domain analysis of a state estimator's output can serve as a lightweight introspective tool to complement existing robustness techniques in real-world robotic deployments, and opens promising avenues for future investigation.

</details>


### [133] [OAA: Three Phases of Vocal Guidance in Human-Drone Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10651)
*Allan Henry,Christian Graff,Solange Rossato,José-Ernesto Gomez-Balderas,Sylvain Huet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究人类语音引导的空间指导行为，通过运动捕捉和语音数据发现语音引导在任务进程中稳定地组织为三个不同阶段（Orientation、Approach、Adjustment），并讨论其对语音引导遥操作自适应控制的意义。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。论文主题为voice-guided teleoperation（语音引导遥操作），涉及人机交互、语音识别与运动轨迹分析，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关。不涉及object detection、remote sensing、pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS、FLOPs等任何目标检测或轻量化内容。唯一勉强相关的点是"drone teleoperation"（无人机遥操作），但研究的是人类语音引导行为学，而非机载感知或模型部署。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为语音引导遥操作行为学，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，无借鉴价值，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语音引导遥操作中的人类空间指导行为分析（human spatial guidance）。应用场景：人-人引导（finger pointing）与人-无人机遥操作（gamepad control）两种配置。传感器类型：motion capture（运动捕捉）、speech data（语音数据）。目标类别：非视觉目标检测，而是语音/运动行为学分析。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构。方法核心为change point detection（变点检测）用于3D轨迹信号分段，以及Kruskal-Wallis统计检验验证阶段差异。属于统计信号处理与行为学分析方法，非深度学习网络架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性指标。虽然涉及无人机遥操作，但讨论的是人类语音引导行为学，而非机载推理部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：两种实验配置——human-human guidance（finger pointing，N=10 dyads）和human-drone teleoperation（gamepad control，N=29 dyads）。评价指标：Kruskal-Wallis检验（p<.001）用于统计验证阶段差异；change point detection用于自动识别阶段边界；inter-utterance silence（话语间静默）作为边界线索。

Experiments: 主要发现：自发语音引导稳定组织为三个阶段（Orientation、Approach、Adjustment）；三个词汇族（rotation vocabulary、translation vocabulary、attenuators）在不同配置间复现；rotation词汇标记Orientation阶段，translation词汇在该阶段稀缺，attenuators向Adjustment阶段累积；inter-utterance silence标记Orientation边界（speech rate单独无法标记）；两种配置（不同电机接口）均出现相同三阶段结构，表明这是人类空间指导的内在属性而非实验伪影。

Limitations: 与本研究方向（遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化）基本无关。摘要未说明任何与遥感、目标检测、模型压缩、部署相关的信息。论文聚焦人类语音引导行为学，属于人机交互/认知科学范畴，对遥感在轨智能无直接借鉴价值。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接启发：论文提出的"阶段化/变点检测"思想（将连续过程划分为不同阶段）在概念上可类比于遥感目标检测中的多阶段处理或场景理解，但方法论上（语音行为学）与遥感视觉检测无实质关联。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Voice-guided teleoperation requires systems that adapt to the evolving dynamics of human guidance. Yet most voice-controlled robot systems treat spoken commands as a stationary stream, ignoring how the guide's communicative behavior changes as the task progresses. Using motion capture and speech data from two experimental configurations, humanhuman guidance (finger pointing, N =10 dyads) and humandrone teleoperation (gamepad control, N =29 dyads), we show that spontaneous vocal guidance consistently organizes into three kinematically and linguistically distinct phases: Orientation, Approach, and Adjustment. These phases are identified automatically via change point detection on 3D trajectory signals, and validated statistically (Kruskal-Wallis, p<.001). Three lexical families replicate across configurations: rotation vocabulary marks Orientation, translation vocabulary is scarce there, and attenuators accumulate toward Adjustment. Together with inter-utterance silence, these cues mark the Orientation boundary that speech rate alone leaves unmarked. The same three-phase structure emerges in both configurations despite radically different motor interfaces, suggesting it is an intrinsic property of human spatial guidance rather than an artifact of the experimental setup. We discuss implications for OAA-aware adaptive control in voice-guided teleoperation.

</details>


### [134] [Robust Sliding Mode and Admittance Control of Underactuated Aerial Manipulators for Contact-Based Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10656)
*Tareq Aziz Alqutami,Yvan Petillot,Matthew W. Dunnigan,Mustafa Suphi Erden*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于滑模控制与导纳控制的欠驱动六旋翼机械臂鲁棒控制框架，用于接触式工业检测中的力调节与轨迹跟踪。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于无人机（aerial platform）的接触式工业检测控制问题，属于机器人控制领域，不涉及遥感（remote sensing）、卫星（satellite）、地球观测（Earth observation）、目标检测（object detection）或模型轻量化（lightweight model）等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：接触式工业检测中的力控制与轨迹跟踪；应用场景：工业检测（contact-based industrial inspection）；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 控制框架：积分增强滑模控制（Integral-augmented SMC）用于轨迹跟踪 + 导纳控制（admittance control）用于力调节；机械臂：1-DoF manipulator；平台：欠驱动六旋翼（underactuated hexarotor）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验方式：Software-in-the-loop仿真；评价指标：轨迹跟踪性能（与PID对比）、力调节RMSE（0.12N）、最大稳定接触力（20N）；无标准遥感数据集。

Experiments: SMC方法相比传统PID在轨迹跟踪和耦合抑制上表现更优；力调节RMSE为0.12N，可稳定施加20N接触力。

Limitations: 仅进行了软件在环仿真，缺乏实物实验验证；未讨论计算资源需求或实时推理约束；与遥感目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于无人机控制领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Contact-based industrial inspection requires aerial platforms to maintain stable interaction while rejecting disturbances. Underactuated aerial manipulators present control challenges due to the dynamic coupling between vehicle attitude and force generation. This paper proposes a robust control framework for an underactuated hexarotor equipped with a 1-DoF manipulator to perform sustained contact inspection. The architecture integrates integral-augmented Sliding Mode Control (SMC) for trajectory tracking with an admittance control law for force regulation. The contact force is mapped to a feedforward attitude term, while the 1-DoF arm actively compensates for the tilt to maintain surface alignment. Software-in-the-loop simulations demonstrate that the SMC-based approach achieves superior tracking and coupling rejection compared to traditional PID. Furthermore, the interaction strategy achieved precise force regulation with an RMSE of 0.12N and was able to stably exert up to 20N force, confirming the system's efficacy for stable, reliable contact-based inspection.

</details>


### [135] [TCAM for Autonomous Deformable Manipulation: The RMC2 Champion System for WBCD 2026 Track 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10718)
*Guangrui Shen,Zhili He,Shigang Wang,Yuanjun Sun,Qing Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是RMC2团队在WBCD 2026 Track 4可变形操作挑战赛中的冠军方案技术报告，围绕TCAM框架构建了一套全自主机器人系统，用于从堆叠中拾取T恤、装载到印花托盘并对齐衣领和抚平印花区域，最终以约23秒/次的速度装载25件T恤并获第一名。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。该论文是机器人可变形物体操作（deformable manipulation）任务，涉及机器人抓取、布料操作、多视角感知与VLA策略，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等遥感检测任务；更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等模型压缩与部署技术。因此与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文为机器人可变形操作竞赛方案，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型压缩部署技术，阅读价值很低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人可变形物体操作（deformable manipulation），具体为从堆叠中拾取单件T恤、装载到印花托盘、对齐衣领、抚平印花区域。应用场景：工业机器人/竞赛场景（WBCD 2026 Track 4）。传感器类型：多视角RGB相机（双上肢鱼眼相机+双下肢RGB相机，共四相机）。目标类别：T恤（布料/可变形物体）。

Model architecture: 核心框架为TCAM（TermiBrain Causal Action Model），采用多视角VLA（Vision-Language-Action）骨干网络，输出30步末端执行器delta-pose动作块（action chunks）。系统包含自定义3D打印夹爪（用于单层布料分离）、双机械臂ARX X5平台、腕部四相机设置。TCAM形成闭环：分析每条轨迹的物理因素→驱动定向数据重采集与策略微调。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（如剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。其"轻量化"相关概念仅体现在通过硬件、感知、数据、学习的联合设计来降低策略需处理的物理交互复杂度，而非模型层面的轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅给出竞赛性能：平均约23秒/次装载，25件中22件达到所需表面平滑度。该性能指标针对机器人操作速度，与星上/边缘部署的实时推理指标无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据：结合便携式UMI风格演示数据与在可部署平台上采集的真实机器人演示数据，提供广泛操作先验与部署特定动力学。评价指标：装载成功率（25件中22件达到表面平滑度）、平均操作时间（约23秒/次）。竞赛：WBCD 2026 Track 4。

Experiments: 在最终竞赛中，系统以约23秒/次的平均速度装载25件T恤，其中22件达到所需表面平滑度，在Track 4中获得第一名。摘要未提供消融实验或与其他方法的定量对比。

Limitations: 摘要未说明模型参数量、计算量、推理延迟等部署指标；未提供消融实验；未说明泛化能力（如不同布料、不同堆叠状态）；未说明失败案例（3件未达平滑度）的原因分析；未涉及任何遥感或目标检测场景。

Ideas for my research: 尽管与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，但可借鉴其"硬件-感知-数据-学习联合降低任务复杂度"的系统设计思想：在星上部署中，可通过传感器配置、数据采集策略与模型设计的协同来降低在轨推理负担。此外，其"闭环分析轨迹物理因素→定向数据重采集与策略微调"的迭代优化思路，可类比为在轨模型基于真实星上数据反馈进行持续微调与校准的机制。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: This technical report describes the RMC2 Team's champion solution for the WBCD 2026 Track 4: Deformable Manipulation Challenge. The task requires a robot to pick a single T-shirt from a stack, load it onto a printing pallet, align the collar with a target area, and smooth the printing region, a sequence that involves single-layer separation, deformable transport, precise placement, and contact-rich surface adjustment. The competition strongly incentivizes fully autonomous execution, motivating the development of an autonomous solution. We built a fully autonomous system around the TCAM (TermiBrain Causal Action Model) framework, with the design principle that hardware, perception, data, and learning should jointly reduce the physical interaction complexity the policy must handle. A custom 3D-printed gripper designed for single-layer fabric separation improves picking reliability on a dual-arm ARX X5 platform. A wrist-centric four-camera setup pairs upper fisheye cameras for task-level context with lower RGB cameras for close-range gripper-cloth contact observation. We combine portable UMI-style demonstrations with real-robot demonstrations collected on the deployable platform to provide both broad manipulation priors and deployment-specific dynamics. TCAM ties these components into a closed loop: each trajectory is analyzed to identify the physical factors contributing to its outcome, driving targeted data recollection and policy fine-tuning. The policy outputs 30-step end-effector delta-pose action chunks from a multi-view VLA backbone. In the final competition, our system loaded 25 T-shirts at an average of approximately 23 seconds per attempt, with 22 achieving the required surface smoothness, securing first place in Track 4.

</details>


### [136] [Embodied Multimodal Grounding for Open-Vocabulary Mobile Manipulation via Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10756)
*Huosen Ou,Dongni Song,Yuncong Wang,Tao Zhou,Yiding Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种融合主动多视角语义3D高斯泼溅(Semantic-3DGS)、可达性感知基座定位和扩散视觉-语言-动作策略的具身多模态抓取框架，用于开放词汇目标定位与少样本操作。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于具身机器人移动操作(embodied mobile manipulation)，涉及视觉-语言-动作对齐、3D场景理解、抓取规划等，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关。不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解。不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT。与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：开放词汇目标定位与少样本操作(open-vocabulary target grounding with few-shot manipulation)；场景：本地家庭工作空间(local household workspaces)；传感器：RGB视觉(多视图)；目标类别：家庭常见物体。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 主动多视角Semantic-3DGS (Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting)作为共享接口；(2) 可达性感知基座定位(reachability-aware base positioning)；(3) 扩散视觉-语言-动作策略(diffusion-based vision-language-action policy)。3D语义线索仅注入到动作专家块的后端( late action-expert blocks)以保留预训练动作先验。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 评估方式：50次真实机器人试验(50-trial real-robot evaluations)；指标：长时程成功率(long-horizon success)、杂乱场景成功率、高度偏移下成功率、消除虚假抓取率。未使用遥感数据集。

Experiments: 对比方法：PointVLA (40%长时程成功率)、DexVLA (28%)；本方法达到60%长时程成功率。在重度杂乱操作中达74%成功率(单视图变体52%，PointVLA 46%)。在75cm高度偏移下保持75%成功率，消除照片诱导的虚假抓取。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但该工作面向家庭机器人操作，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测在任务场景、传感器类型、部署约束上完全不同，缺乏直接可迁移性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：(1) Semantic-3DGS作为多模态共享接口的思想，若迁移到遥感多模态(光学+SAR+高光谱)融合检测中可能有参考价值；(2) 将语义线索仅注入后端模块以保留预训练先验的策略，类似于轻量化中保持骨干网络权重、仅微调检测头的思路。但整体方向差异过大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Embodied mobile manipulation requires language, visual observations, three-dimensional scene structure, and action feasibility to be aligned before execution. We study open-vocabulary target grounding with few-shot manipulation in local household workspaces and present an embodied multimodal grounding framework that integrates active multi-view Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (Semantic-3DGS), reachability-aware base positioning, and a diffusion-based vision-language-action policy. A task-driven local Semantic-3DGS serves as a shared interface across active sensing, language-conditioned 3D localization, obstacle-aware scene reasoning, base preparation, and semantic conditioning of the action model. To preserve pretrained action priors, the 3D semantic cues are injected only into the late action-expert blocks. In expanded 50-trial real-robot evaluations against representative vision-language-action (VLA) approaches, the full system achieves 60% long-horizon success compared with 40% for PointVLA and 28% for DexVLA, and reaches 74% success in heavily cluttered manipulation compared with 52% for the single-view variant and 46% for PointVLA. It also maintains 75% success under a 75 cm height shift and eliminates photo-induced false grasps. These results indicate that explicit, refreshable 3D semantic grounding can improve robustness under clutter, occlusion, viewpoint variation, and embodiment constraints.

</details>


### [137] [JEPA-WAM: Stage-Level Joint-Embedding Prediction for World-Action Models in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10780)
*Xiao Liu,Yuguang Yang,Xi Wang,Kai Jiang,Cheng Chi,Yong Xu,Wenchao Ding,Yilun Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出JEPA-WAM，一种结合Stage-JEPA预测器与World Action Model的通用机器人策略，用于多模态任务指令到动作的映射，在50个RoboTwin 2.0任务上取得90.25%成功率。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦通用机器人操作策略（generalist robot policy），涉及多模态观测与语言指令到动作的映射，属于机器人操作（robot manipulation）领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：通用机器人操作策略（generalist robot policy），具体为机器人操作（robot manipulation）。应用场景：RoboTwin 2.0仿真环境中的50个任务（clean and randomized environments）。传感器类型：多模态观测（multimodal observations）。目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明具体传感器类型。

Model architecture: JEPA-WAM：基于Motus的World Action Model (WAM) 结合Stage-JEPA。Stage-JEPA是一种目标条件化的联合嵌入预测架构（goal-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture, JEPA）预测器。使用冻结的V-JEPA2编码器提取当前状态表示，并预测下一推断阶段的潜在目标。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：RoboTwin 2.0（50个任务，包含clean和randomized两种环境）。评价指标：总体成功率（overall success rate），成功rollout的平均执行步数（mean number of execution steps in successful rollouts）。

Experiments: 在50个RoboTwin 2.0任务上，JEPA-WAM达到90.25%总体成功率，相比最强基线，成功rollout的平均执行步数减少5.97%。摘要未说明具体对比方法名称。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。2. 仅在仿真环境RoboTwin 2.0中验证，未涉及真实机器人或遥感场景。3. 未讨论模型效率、推理速度、资源消耗等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。其JEPA架构中的联合嵌入预测思想（预测潜在空间中的目标表示）在理论上可迁移到遥感时序预测或变化检测任务，但与本课题核心方向（目标检测+轻量化）关联微弱。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies aim to map multimodal observations and linguistic task instructions to actions across diverse tasks. However, existing methods typically represent the future as a fixed, short video-action chunk. This short-term future captures local scene evolution for action execution, but it does not explicitly describe the stage-level future that specifies how a task should progress from its current stage to the next. We therefore distinguish two complementary futures for robot manipulation: a short-term physical future to capture local scene evolution and a stage-level semantic future to represent task progress. We introduce JEPA-WAM, which augments a Motus-based World Action Model (WAM) with Stage-JEPA, a goal-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) predictor. Given the current observation and task instruction, Stage-JEPA uses a frozen V-JEPA2 encoder to extract the current-state representation and predicts the latent target of the next inferred stage. Across 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks in clean and randomized environments, JEPA-WAM achieves 90.25% overall success and reduces the mean number of execution steps in successful rollouts by 5.97% relative to the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [138] [Dual Stress: Runtime Safety Monitoring for Safety-Constrained MPC Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10791)
*Jamil Chahine,Wenqi Cai,John Abanes,Anthony Tzes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究自动驾驶导航中的运行时危险监控器，利用模型预测控制中的KKT乘子构建"对偶应力"信号作为几何危险信号的补充，不涉及遥感、卫星或目标检测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于自动驾驶导航中的危险监控，使用模型预测控制(MPC)的KKT乘子构建危险信号，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自动驾驶导航危险监控；应用场景：自动驾驶车辆；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：障碍物（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: 基于模型预测控制(MPC)的约束优化框架，利用KKT乘子计算"对偶应力"信号。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用物理仿真器生成的预注册留出交叉场景(preregistered held-out crossing scenarios)；评价指标：碰撞检测率、误报率(false-alarm budget)。

Experiments: 与15种几何检测器对比，在匹配的误报预算下，对偶应力信号比整个几何检测器组合多检测到4.7倍被遗漏的碰撞（85 vs 18）；两种通道联合可预警四分之三的碰撞，而单独几何检测器仅预警不到一半。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：在约束优化框架中利用对偶变量（如KKT乘子）作为辅助信息通道的思路，但与本课题的遥感目标检测轻量化方向差距过大。

Abstract: Runtime hazard monitors for autonomous naviga- tion are conventionally built from geometric quantities: predicted clearance, time to collision, and required deceleration. A model-predictive controller that enforces safety through explicit con- straints computes, as a by-product of every control step, a second information channel that such monitors ignore: the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker multipliers of its constrained optimization, which measure the marginal control effort spent to maintain safety against each obstacle. This paper evaluates whether a horizon-weighted sum of those multipliers, a dual stress signal, provides a hazard monitor complementary to the geometric warnings the same state already supports. We compare it against a battery of fifteen geometric detectors tuned to a matched false-alarm budget, on preregistered held-out crossing scenarios driven through a physics simulator. The stress alarm actionably flags 4.7 times as many collisions missed by the entire geometric battery as the geometric battery flags in return (85 versus 18); combined, the two channels warn of three quarters of the collisions for which braking remained feasible, against under half for the geometric battery alone.

</details>


### [139] [AECNav: Active Evidence Consolidation for Efficient Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10817)
*Guanlin Liu,Shaobin Ling,Renyuan Liu,Zeying Gong,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Research relevance: Relevance analysis unavailable

Reading priority: Priority unavailable

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation (ZSON) in open-vocabulary scenarios is challenging, as it requires a robot to locate an arbitrarily specified object in an unseen environment without task-specific training. Currently, the task still suffers from high latency and limited accuracy due to redundant perception pipelines and insufficient evidence for reliable target confirmation. In this letter, we reframe ZSON as an evidence-driven perception-to-decision problem and present AECNav, a training-free pipeline built on three components: i) Evidence-gated perception, which utilizes a shared encoding across all reasoning stages to establish a unified semantic basis and eliminate redundant computations; ii) Evidence consolidation, which aggregates detections into cluster-level log-odds beliefs. This explicitly separates genuine target support from the false confidence of visually similar distractors, while treating the absence of expected detections as negative evidence; and iii) Active evidence acquisition, which sustains productive exploration under weak semantic cues by selecting frontiers that maximize information gain at minimal traversal cost. As a result, AECNav significantly outperforms previous methods and achieves state-of-the-art success rates of 84.7%, 57.3%, and 51.3% on HM3D-v2, HM3D-OVON, and MP3D, respectively, with substantially lower inference overhead, and attains 95% success across 40 trials on a physical quadruped robot at roughly 5Hz. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [140] [Neural Introspection Gating for Adaptive KV-Cache Reuse in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10824)
*Zhijie Wu,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Gated VLA-Cache，一种无需训练的轻量扩展，通过神经内省（监测top-2动作token的logit margin）来动态决定是否使KV缓存失效并触发全量重算，在VLA实时控制中兼顾计算节省与可靠性。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于泛视觉-语言-动作（VLA）机器人控制领域，核心是KV缓存复用与置信度门控，与遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测均无直接关系。其"轻量、训练-free、缓存复用+不确定性门控"的思想对轻量化推理有方法层面的借鉴意义，但场景完全不同。

Reading priority: 2分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向场景不匹配，但"KV缓存复用+置信度门控"的推理期加速思想对时序遥感图像的自适应计算有一定借鉴价值，可作泛读参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：VLA模型实时控制（vision-language-action），将相机图像和语言指令映射为电机命令。场景：机器人操作（LIBERO基准）。传感器：相机图像。目标类别：动作token（非目标检测）。

Model architecture: 基于自回归transformer的VLA模型（OpenVLA、OpenVLA-OFT），采用KV缓存机制复用相邻帧视觉token的key-value表示。Gated VLA-Cache在视觉相似性缓存基础上增加神经内省门控模块。

Lightweight method: 训练-free的缓存复用加速：复用视觉静态patch的KV状态避免重复计算；通过logit margin（top-2动作token置信度差）作为零成本置信度信号，低于阈值时使缓存失效并触发全量重算。属于推理期加速/缓存技术，非剪枝、量化、蒸馏或NAS。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅提及"retaining 80% of the compute savings"（保留80%的计算节省），但未给出绝对数值。与星上/边缘部署无直接关联。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LIBERO基准的四个套件（LIBERO-Goal、LIBERO-Long等）。模型：OpenVLA和OpenVLA-OFT。指标：准确率（accuracy）与计算节省（compute savings）。

Experiments: 在LIBERO-Goal和LIBERO-Long上，当盲缓存（blind caching）损害性能时，Gated VLA-Cache恢复了超过100%的丢失准确率，同时保留80%的计算节省。摘要未给出与其他方法的详细对比表或消融细节。

Limitations: 摘要未说明：具体参数量/FLOPs/延迟数值、门控阈值如何设定、对遥感/目标检测场景的适用性、缓存失效带来的额外重算开销、在非LIBERO场景的泛化性。且方法针对VLA动作token，与目标检测的类别/框回归输出差异较大。

Ideas for my research: 1. "缓存复用+不确定性门控"思想可迁移到遥感在轨目标检测：相邻帧/相邻轨道图像中背景patch变化小，可复用特征或检测结果，用logit margin或检测置信度作为门控决定是否重算，兼顾实时性与精度。2. 零成本置信度信号（logit margin）可作为轻量化检测模型的自适应推理策略，避免对每帧全量计算。3. 训练-free的推理期加速思路适合星上部署（无需重新训练、算力受限）。4. 可借鉴其"在缓存节省与可靠性之间权衡"的评估框架，用于遥感小目标检测的时序复用场景。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action(VLA) models map camera images and language instructions directly to motor commands through a single autoregressive transformer. In real-time control, they still spend substantial compute recomputing key-value(KV) representations for visual tokens that barely change across neighboring frames. Recent work such as VLA-Cache reduces that cost by reusing KV states for visually static patches, but its policy relies only on observation-space heuristics and does not account for the model's own uncertainty. We propose Gated VLA-Cache, a lightweight, training-free extension that augments visual-similarity caching with neural introspection. The method monitors the logit margin between the top two predicted action tokens, a zero-cost confidence signal available during decoding. When the margin drops below a threshold, the cache is invalidated and a full recompute is triggered. Evaluated on four LIBERO benchmark suites with both OpenVLA and OpenVLA-OFT, Gated VLA-Cache improves reliability when blind caching hurts. On LIBERO-Goal and LIBERO-Long, it recovers over 100% of the lost accuracy while retaining 80% of the compute savings.

</details>


### [141] [Enabling Scalable Kinesthetic Teaching via Observer-based Hand-guiding with Active Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10847)
*Anna Tuma,Giuseppe Monetti,Jochen J. Steil,Niels Dehio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RHOAS，一种基于模型力估计的机器人手引导方案，无需额外力传感器即可主动辅助操作者意图运动，降低物理疲劳。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于机器人示教/人机交互领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人手引导（hand-guiding）示教；应用场景：工业机器人模仿学习与编程；传感器：KUKA LWR iiwa机器人关节扭矩传感器；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于模型的外部扭矩估计器，结合内部关节扭矩传感和冗余运动学，主动支持人类物理输入。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 用户实验：16名参与者，KUKA LWR iiwa机器人；评价指标：物理努力程度（主观评分）、可操控性（精确/敏捷任务表现）、用户偏好。

Experiments: 用户研究表明：相比传统无辅助手引导方法，RHOAS在物理努力、可操控性和用户偏好上均有统计显著改善。

Limitations: 1. 依赖精确的机器人动力学模型，未建模的关节弹性动力学和测量噪声会影响估计精度；2. 运动学奇异点附近估计精度下降；3. 静态重力补偿误差需要额外处理；4. 仅在单一机器人平台上验证。

Ideas for my research: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化课题基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。

Abstract: Kinesthetic teaching through robot hand-guiding provides a natural interface for collecting demonstrations in imitation learning and programming-by-demonstration. However, extended sessions cause operator fatigue, reducing demonstration quality and limiting scalability. Current industrial hand-guiding approaches typically provide no active assistance, and alternatives require costly wrist-mounted force-torque sensors or rely on learned motion priors unavailable for new tasks. We propose RHOAS, a hand-guiding scheme that actively supports operator-intended motions using model-based force estimation without additional hardware. Our approach considers robot hand-guiding as an actively controlled interaction by the human operator, rather than an interaction with a passive environment. Standard methods used for hand-guiding typically rely on general passivity-based compliant control architectures that unnecessarily increase operator effort and limit the range of demonstrable motions without providing the intended stability guarantees in active interaction. Instead, our design utilizes model-based external torque estimation, internal joint torque sensing, and redundant robot kinematics to actively support human physical input within the human interaction frequency bandwidth. We address practical challenges of relying on observer-based force estimation, including suppression of unmodeled joint elastic dynamic effects and measurement noise in the feedback path, reduced estimate accuracy close to kinematic singularities, and static gravity compensation errors. In a user study with 16 participants on a KUKA LWR iiwa we demonstrate statistically significant reductions in physical effort, improved maneuverability for both precise and agile tasks, and clear user preference.

</details>


### [142] [Flex-$π$: A Multi-Stream World-Action Model with Compute Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10860)
*Ge Yan,Jinghao Liu,Yuzhi Fan,Lei Cai,Minwen Liao,Jesse Zhang,Dieter Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Flex-π，一个 6B 参数的 world-action model (WAM)，利用冻结的视频生成 VAE 隐空间同时编码 RGB、3D pointmaps 和 DINO 语义，在 Mixture-of-Transformers 骨干中联合去噪，实现演示高效且泛化强的双臂灵巧操作策略。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2 分）。该论文是机器人操作领域的 world-action model / 视觉-动作策略方法，属于泛视觉与泛机器学习方法。它不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱或多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏等轻量化方法。其核心贡献在于多模态隐空间联合建模与跨模态 dropout 的架构设计，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1 分。该论文为机器人操作 world-action model，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测或模型压缩方法，仅架构层面的多模态隐空间思想有极弱借鉴意义，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：world-action model 预测未来以指导机器人动作（双臂灵巧操作）。场景：真实世界 bimanual manipulation（双臂操作）。传感器：RGB 相机（视频生成 VAE 编码）。目标类别：非目标检测，而是机器人操作动作策略。摘要未说明具体传感器类型细节。

Model architecture: Flex-π 为 6B 参数 WAM。核心：冻结的 video-generation VAE 隐空间同时编码 RGB、3D pointmaps 和 DINO 语义；所有视觉信号投影到共享隐空间，在 Mixture-of-Transformers 骨干中与动作联合去噪；per-stream dropout + cross-modality forcing 使单一 checkpoint 可运行任意流子集（从纯 action-only 到完整联合生成）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明传统轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS 等）。唯一与"轻量/高效"相关的点是：通过 per-stream dropout 与 cross-modality forcing，单一 checkpoint 可运行在 action-only 快速模式，从而降低推理开销（"running faster than π0.5"）。但这属于运行时模态裁剪，而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量部署细节（除 6B 参数外）、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等。仅提及推理速度快于 π0.5 基线。6B 参数规模对星上/边缘部署而言过大，且面向机器人操作而非遥感在轨推理，不具备直接星上部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：在真实世界双臂灵巧、精确操作任务上，in-distribution 与 out-of-distribution 均超越最强基线 2-7×（演示效率/泛化能力）。摘要未说明具体指标名称（如成功率、平均完成时间等）。

Experiments: 主要实验：真实世界双臂灵巧操作任务，in-distribution 与 out-of-distribution 场景，Flex-π 相比最强基线提升 2-7×，且推理速度快于 π0.5。摘要未说明消融实验细节。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。可推断：6B 参数规模巨大，部署成本高；面向机器人操作而非遥感场景；未涉及遥感目标检测、小目标/旋转框检测、轻量化压缩等；无遥感数据集与指标验证。

Ideas for my research: 尽管与遥感在轨检测弱相关，仍有少量可借鉴的架构思想：(1) 共享隐空间 + 多模态联合建模的思路，可类比遥感多光谱/高光谱与 RGB 的联合表征；(2) per-stream dropout + cross-modality forcing 的"单一模型多模态子集运行"思想，可启发遥感在轨模型在不同传感器/波段子集下自适应运行，从而在特定任务下降低计算开销；(3) 冻结预训练 VAE 隐空间作为"免费午餐"复用多模态信息的思路，可类比复用预训练遥感特征。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为不涉及目标检测与模型压缩核心。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) predict the future to act better, but nearly all of them predict only RGB latents, trained purely for pixel reconstruction, with no explicit signal for the 3D geometry or object semantics manipulation needs. We find a surprising free lunch: the same frozen video-generation VAE that encodes RGB also encodes 3D pointmaps almost losslessly, with no pointmap-specific training at all. This lets us supervise Flex-$π$, a 6B-parameter WAM, on 3D geometry and object-centric DINO semantics alongside RGB, at no cost in new sensors, new pre-training, or inference latency. Every visual signal is projected into this shared latent space and denoised jointly with actions inside a Mixture-of-Transformers backbone; per-stream dropout with cross-modality forcing then lets a single trained checkpoint run on any subset of these streams, from a fast action-only mode to full joint generation. The result is a policy that is exceptionally demonstration-efficient and generalizes well, beating the strongest baselines by up to 2-7$\times$ on dexterous, precise, real-world bimanual manipulation tasks both in and out of distribution, all while running faster than $π_{0.5}$. Our project website: https://flex-pi.github.io/

</details>


### [143] [Aerial Layouting: Design and Control of a Compliant and Actuated End-Effector for Precise In-flight Marking on Ceilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10987)
*Christian Lanegger,Marco Ruggia,Marco Tognon,Lionel Ott,Roland Siegwart*

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Aerial robots have demonstrated impressive feats of precise control, such as dynamic flight through openings or highly complex choreographies. Despite the accuracy needed for these tasks, there are problems that require levels of precision that are challenging to achieve today. One such problem is aerial interaction. Advances in aerial robot design and control have made such contact-based tasks possible and opened up research into challenging real-world tasks, including contact-based inspection. However, while centimetre accuracy is sufficient and achievable for inspection tasks, the positioning accuracy needed for other problems, such as layouting on construction sites or general push-and-slide tasks, is millimetres. To achieve such a high precision, we propose a new aerial system composed of an aerial vehicle equipped with a novel "smart" end-effector leveraging a stability-optimized Gough-Stewart mechanism. We present its design process and features incorporating the principles of compliance, multiple contact points, actuation, and self-containment. In experiments, we verify that the design choices made for our novel end-effector are necessary to obtain the desired positioning precision. Furthermore, we demonstrate that we can reliably mark lines on ceilings with millimetre accuracy without the need for precise modeling or sophisticated control of the aerial robot.

</details>

Abstract: Advancing autonomy for surface vessels requires systematic evaluation of their sensing and perception subsystems. Yet, maritime environments impose unique challenges: sensor installation is constrained by vessel layout, environmental conditions such as fog or sea clutter are difficult to reproduce, and long-duration missions complicate data collection. This work addresses the question: How can we design a modular and reproducible sensor platform for maritime autonomy?
  We present a comprehensive design blueprint that incorporates diverse modalities - RADAR, LiDAR, IMU, GNSS, AIS, RGB and LWIR cameras, and weather sensors - to enhance environmental awareness and vessel proprioception. Supported by a dedicated ROS2-based software framework for data management, our modular platform enables long-term data collection, hardware-in-the-loop testing, and integration with existing sensors and algorithms. By unifying hardware design and data capture methodology, the platform enhances reproducibility and comparability across vessels and research projects. The proposed framework bridges engineering implementation and research methodology, providing the foundation for standardized, verifiable datasets essential to advancing situational awareness and autonomous maritime navigation.

</details>

Abstract: The tight coupling of subsystems in most robots, though a natural consequence of their complexity, leads to monolithic designs that are time-consuming and difficult to adapt after initial deployment. To address this challenge, we present a framework and supporting abstractions for recomposition during runtime that enable robots to quickly integrate previously unseen modular software, hardware, and compute payloads. Our approach allows non-expert users to quickly add new capabilities in the field through a true plug-and-play process. Crucially, new resources are not only immediately available to a host robot but are also shared with distributed peers, enabling compute-constrained systems to access powerful new remote capabilities. Our framework reduces reconfiguration time to a matter of minutes with no developer intervention, in stark contrast to the hours of expert effort often required for traditional manual integration. We demonstrate our method in two disaster response scenarios, including radioactive source localization at an operational nuclear reactor facility and a thermal-guided search for people in dark, difficult-to-reach spaces. These demonstrations show how in-field recomposition provides timely, flexible, and accessible adaptation to dynamic requirements, representing a critical step toward creating robots that can quickly evolve alongside the tasks, technologies, and environments they support.

</details>


### [146] [VIScore: Diagnosing Planning-Relevant Quality in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11174)
*Haiyu Wu,Randall Balestriero,Morgan Levine*

Abstract: Regulating the latent space to an isotropic Gaussian distribution provides a stable and information-maximized landscape for world model planning. However, the latent space property and successful planning remain disconnected. We first study this by comparing SIGReg and VISReg, two regularization loss functions with the same distribution target but different properties. Compared with SIGReg, VISReg has more flexibility in controlling the weights of center, scale, and shape regularization, and a larger batch size brings a finer distribution approximation. We find that the former, despite being beneficial in self-supervised learning (SSL), does not help the planning, whereas the latter improves the planning success on out-of-domain (OOD) datasets. This motivates a deep understanding of the factors that correlate with the success rate. Unlike the previous metrics focusing on the encoded latent only, we propose the Veracity-Influence-Sobriety score (VIScore), a metric that quantifies the reachability and capacity of a predictor given the encoded feature, and the hallucination of the searching-based planner. Compared with straightness, physical-state probing, and empowerment, we show that, with the measurement covering encoder, predictor, and planner, VIScore explains the success rate better than the others, as reflected by a strong Spearman correlation. Specifically, VIScore consistently achieves a Spearman correlation over 0.75 on both seen and unseen models and datasets on the cross-task success rate pool. Moreover, VIScore is the only metric that has a calibration error below the constant fit across all testing scenarios, showcasing the importance of these three aspects in planning success. We hope this metric can help future studies on world model design and diagnosis.

</details>

Abstract: For autonomous space exploration, robotic agents need to perform motion planning in which environmental interactions may be unknown. Learning these interactions, such as terrain mechanics for wheeled robots, can introduce uncertainties that lead to risky motion plans and potentially hazardous operations or mission failures. Moreover, uncertainties induced by perception-based systems can exacerbate the problem of safe motion planning. In this letter, we address the problem of performing cost-optimal kinodynamic motion planning with risk awareness. We approach this in two steps. First, a sampling-based planner (AO-RRT) generates a dynamically feasible, risk-aware, and asymptotically cost-optimal trajectory. Second, we formulate motion planning as a nonlinear optimization problem and solve it using sequential convex programming (SCP), using the AO-RRT trajectory as an initial solution. By quantifying risk using conditional value-at-risk (CVaR), we demonstrate a reduction in risk by over $\sim$97\% across trajectories in simulation and hardware experiments.

</details>


### [148] [Surgical WAM: A World-Action Model for Data-Efficient Surgical Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11204)
*Wenrui Bao,Tianyun Jiang,Zhiben Chen,Ser-Nam Lim,Peter D. Peng,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Surgical WAM模型，利用无动作标签的内窥镜视频预训练学习手术场景视觉动态，再在少量有动作标签数据上微调，实现闭环手术机器人控制，在四个模拟手术任务上提升成功率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于手术机器人（surgical robot）的操控策略学习，场景为内窥镜手术（endoscopic surgery），传感器为内窥镜视频，目标为手术器械操控。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化/部署相关方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：手术机器人操控策略学习（closed-loop surgical manipulation）；应用场景：模拟手术环境（four simulated surgical manipulation tasks）；传感器类型：内窥镜视频（endoscopic video）；目标类别：手术器械操控动作，非目标检测任务。

Model architecture: Surgical WAM (Surgical World-Action Model)，基于Cosmos Policy构建的统一生成模型，联合预测未来内窥镜观测和可执行的手术机器人动作块（action chunks）。采用receding-horizon控制策略，执行预测动作块的前缀并基于新观测重新规划。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：四个模拟手术操控任务（包括PegTransfer等），使用内窥镜视频和同步运动学轨迹数据；评价指标：成功率（success rate）。

Experiments: 在四个模拟手术任务上，无动作视频预训练将平均成功率从63.5%提升至77.8%，其中PegTransfer任务绝对提升20个百分点。接触密集和双手协调任务提升最大。

Limitations: 1. 仅在模拟环境中验证，未在真实手术机器人上测试；2. 不涉及遥感/卫星场景；3. 不涉及目标检测任务；4. 不涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化方法。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人课题方向基本无关，但以下思路可抽象借鉴：1. 无标签预训练+少量标签微调的数据高效范式，可类比于遥感卫星在轨场景中利用大量无标注遥感图像进行自监督预训练，再在少量标注目标检测数据上微调；2. 世界模型（world model）思想——预测未来观测，可类比于遥感时序预测或视频目标检测中的时序建模。但需注意，该论文不涉及目标检测、轻量化、星上部署等核心关切。

Abstract: Learning reliable surgical manipulation policies is bottlenecked by the scarcity of action-labeled demonstrations: teleoperated surgical robot (e.g., dVRK) trajectories with synchronized kinematics are costly to collect, while surgical tasks demand precise contact handling, long-horizon reasoning, and bimanual coordination. Endoscopic video is comparatively inexpensive and abundant relative to synchronized video--kinematics trajectories, and a natural way to exploit it is to learn world models of surgical scenes. However, existing surgical world models use video primarily for simulation or policy evaluation, and rarely translate the learned dynamics into closed-loop control. This gap raises our central question: under a fixed budget of action-labeled demonstrations, does action-free video pretraining improve closed-loop surgical manipulation? To answer it, we introduce the Surgical World-Action Model (Surgical WAM), a unified generative model built on Cosmos Policy that jointly predicts future endoscopic observations and executable surgical robot action chunks. Surgical WAM first learns surgical visual dynamics from action-free video and is then fine-tuned on the fixed action-labeled budget; at deployment, it acts as a closed-loop, receding-horizon controller that executes a short prefix of each predicted action chunk and replans from the resulting observation. On a suite of four simulated surgical manipulation tasks, video pretraining improves the average success rate from 63.5% to 77.8%, including an absolute gain of 20 percentage points on PegTransfer, with the largest improvements on contact-rich and bimanual tasks. These results demonstrate that action-free video provides transferable visual dynamics priors for learning surgical robot control with limited action supervision, positioning data-efficient video pretraining as a practical path toward scaling up surgical robot learning.

</details>

### [149] [Closed-Loop LLM Co-Pilots for Digital Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09949)
*Serge Kernbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了大型语言模型（LLM）在复杂生物系统中的应用，从数据分析演进到自主AI引导实验，通过49通道植物传感器网络（多光谱、电化学、介电模态）驱动，实现从人在环分析到自主控制的闭环AI-生物接口，并在垂直农场等场景验证了生产周期缩短35%、能耗降低18%甚至67.9%的效果。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。论文主题是LLM在数字农业/植物生理系统中的应用，属于生物系统与AI控制交叉领域，与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接关联。虽然涉及"multispectral"（多光谱）传感器和"real-time"（实时）自然语言解释，但多光谱在此处指植物生理传感而非遥感影像，且不涉及目标检测、轻量化或星上部署。仅"real-time interpretation"和"lowering computational constraints"与轻量化/实时推理有极弱的间接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为LLM在数字农业/植物生理系统中的应用，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及目标检测、遥感影像或模型压缩技术，仅存在极弱的实时性与计算效率概念关联，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：LLM驱动的自主AI引导实验与植物生理系统闭环控制。应用场景：数字农业、垂直农场、单株植物实验。传感器类型：49通道植物传感器网络（phytosensor network），涵盖multispectral（多光谱）、electrochemical（电化学）、dielectric（介电）模态。目标类别：植物生理指标（生物量积累、叶绿素含量、能耗）。

Model architecture: 核心为Large Language Model（LLM）框架，处理生物物理数据，评估植物生理状态并触发硬件执行器（actuators）以优化微气候、执行表型协议或诱导受控胁迫场景。采用闭环架构（closed-loop architecture），建立直接AI-生物接口。包含多参数优化agent，可调制全光谱、450nm、660nm光照（2小时间隔）。摘要未说明具体LLM模型架构、参数量或网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确涉及传统模型压缩方法（pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS等）。仅提及"lowering computational and expert-labor constraints"（降低计算和专家人力约束）以及"real-time natural-language interpretation"（实时自然语言解释），暗示对计算效率有一定关注，但未给出具体轻量化技术或部署方案。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及"production-scale deployment"（生产规模部署）和"energy consumption"（能耗）作为优化目标之一（能耗降低18%、67.9%），但这里的能耗指农业种植能耗而非模型推理能耗。不涉及星上/边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：基于垂直农场（vertical farm）和单株植物（single-plant setup）的三种案例研究。指标：生产周期缩短35%（minimal-time mode）、能耗降低18%（energy-optimization mode）、67.9%能耗节省（dark-induced chlorophyll accumulation策略）。涉及生物量积累、叶绿素含量、能耗等优化目标。摘要未说明标准机器学习评价指标（如mAP、精度、召回率等）。

Experiments: 在三个案例研究中验证框架：垂直农场和单株植物设置，解析植物生理的微观和宏观波动。生产规模部署中agent执行多参数优化，平衡生物量积累、叶绿素含量和能耗。与周期性控制（periodic control）对比：minimal-time模式将生产周期缩短35%；energy-optimization模式能耗降低18%，仅轻微增加种植时间（利用光脉冲的生理惯性）；agent自主开发了暗诱导叶绿素积累策略，实现67.9%能耗节省。

Limitations: 与本研究方向（遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化）基本无关。摘要未涉及遥感影像、目标检测、小目标/旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测、模型压缩或星上部署等任何相关技术。多光谱在此为植物生理传感而非遥感成像。摘要未说明LLM的具体架构、计算资源需求、推理延迟等关键部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限，但可提取两点间接启发：(1) "real-time natural-language interpretation"和"lowering computational constraints"体现了对实时性与计算效率的追求，与在轨轻量化目标一致，可借鉴其"以最小计算代价实现自主决策"的设计理念；(2) 闭环自主控制架构（sensing→LLM决策→actuator执行）与在轨"感知-决策-执行"自主智能链路有概念上的相似性，可启发在轨目标检测模型与后续自主决策模块的端到端集成设计。但整体上对遥感目标检测轻量化课题的直接可借鉴点很少。

Abstract: This study evaluates the application of Large Language Models (LLMs) in complex biological systems, evolving from data analysis to autonomous, AI-guided experimentation. The framework is driven by data from a 49-channel phytosensor network, encompassing multispectral, electrochemical, and dielectric modalities. To enhance accessibility, the system provides real-time natural-language interpretation for both specialists and non-experts. However, its core advantage lies in the transition from human-in-the-loop analysis to autonomous control. Processing biophysical data, the LLM evaluates plant physiology and triggers hardware actuators to optimize microclimates, execute phenotyping protocols, or induce controlled stress scenarios. This closed-loop architecture establishes a direct AI-biology interface, enabling data-driven exploration of complex biosystems and ecologies. The framework was validated across three case studies, based on a vertical farm and a single-plant setup and deciphered complex micro- and macro-fluctuations in plant physiology. Agents in a production-scale deployment executed multi-parameter optimization, balancing biomass accumulation, chlorophyll content, and energy consumption. The LLM processed biosensing telemetry to modulate full-spectrum, 450 nm, and 660 nm lighting at 2-hour intervals. Compared to periodic control, the system in minimal-time mode reduced the production cycle by 35%. In the energy-optimization mode, it reduced energy consumption by 18% with only a marginal increase in cultivation time, exploiting physiological inertia via light pulses. Finally, the agents autonomously developed an unforeseen strategy of dark-induced chlorophyll accumulation, resulting in a 67.9% energy saving. This framework transforms LLMs into autonomous co-pilots for digital agriculture, improving the cost-to-value ratio and lowering computational and expert-labor constraints.

</details>


### [150] [SPOTting the Future: Lookahead Explanations for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09967)
*Tamar Gozlan,Claudia V. Goldman*

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agents achieve strong performance in complex environments, yet their decision-making processes remain difficult to interpret. We introduce SPOT (Sampling Policy Observation Tree), a novel model-agnostic, sampling-based framework for interpreting DRL policies. Given access to the policy and an environment simulator, SPOT constructs an interpretable finite-horizon tree by sampling actions and recursively simulating the resulting successor states. The tree provides an empirical representation of the policy's action preferences and their possible downstream evolution. We provide formal guarantees establishing SPOT's asymptotic recovery of the policy's unique most probable action and characterizing its disagreement behavior under high-entropy policies. We demonstrate SPOT in the SUMO-RL traffic-signal control domain. The case study illustrates how its tree-based representation can be used to inspect policy preferences, compare alternative future trajectories, and reveal downstream behaviors that are not visible through single-timestep feature-attribution methods.

</details>


### [151] [MIDAS: Mutual Information Disentanglement with Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09986)
*Yuhua Wen,Yingying Zhou,Qifei Li,Yingming Gao,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao,Ya Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Most existing multimodal sentiment analysis approaches assume access to complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world applications frequently encounter incomplete or corrupted modalities, posing a critical challenge. Although several methods have been proposed to tackle this issue, they mainly rely on data imputation and heuristic coordination constraints, which fail to effectively extract and leverage task-relevant information from the incomplete multimodal data. To address this challenge, we propose a unified framework termed Mutual Information Disentanglement with uncertainty-Aware fuSion (MIDAS), which effectively restructures multimodal representations under incomplete conditions. MIDAS adopts a variational modeling strategy to represent each modality with multivariate Gaussian latent variables and further decomposes them into shared and exclusive factors. To obtain reliable representations, we design a minimax objective that minimizes the mutual information between shared and exclusive spaces for stable disentanglement, while maximizing the mutual information among shared spaces across modalities to enhance semantic alignment. In addition, an uncertainty-aware fusion mechanism is introduced, where posterior variance is leveraged as a reliability indicator to adaptively weight latent features during fusion, ensuring robust integration even when modalities are incomplete. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets show that MIDAS achieves strong and consistent performance gains over competitive baselines across a wide range of incomplete settings, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness for incomplete data scenarios.

</details>


### [152] [Towards Sustainable Artificial Intelligence: A Comprehensive Review and Comparative Analysis of Deep Learning Models' Carbon Footprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09998)
*Samar Garrab,Sarra Boughriou,Manel BenSassi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一篇关于绿色AI/绿色深度学习的环境影响综述，包含碳排放测量工具对比和基于CPU的多标签分类实验，强调训练阶段是主要碳排放源，并指出模型复杂度与精度提升不成正比。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文是绿色AI/绿色DL的综述，讨论的是AI模型的碳排放和环境影响，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）技术本身。虽然"轻量化"在绿色AI语境下可能被提及，但本文聚焦的是环境可持续性而非模型压缩加速的工程方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感/卫星场景。任务为多标签分类（multi-label classification），使用CPU实验环境。不涉及遥感图像、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及实现了6个DL模型用于多标签分类，但未给出模型名称或结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文讨论的是绿色AI（Green AI/Green DL）和碳足迹测量，而非模型轻量化技术（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标为分类准确率（accuracy）和碳排放量（carbon emissions）。

Limitations: 1. 实验仅在CPU上进行，未涉及GPU/TPU等实际AI训练常用硬件，结论的普适性有限；2. 仅针对多标签分类任务，未涉及目标检测、语义分割等计算密集型视觉任务；3. 未讨论模型轻量化技术本身，仅从环境角度讨论模型选择；4. 不涉及遥感/卫星场景。

Ideas for my research: 1. 本文提出的"模型复杂度与精度不成正比"的结论，可作为在轨轻量化模型设计时的理论支撑——不必盲目追求大模型，应关注精度-效率帕累托前沿；2. 碳足迹测量工具（carbon measurement tools）可借鉴用于评估星上部署模型的能耗；3. 但整体而言，本文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) have become powerful tools for supporting and automating complex human tasks. Despite their benefits, growing attention has been directed toward their environmental implications, primarily due to their high energy demands and associated carbon emissions. This concern is particularly relevant in light of the increasing deployment of large-scale models, especially Deep Learning (DL) architectures, which provide advanced predictive capabilities but require substantial computational resources. This paper presents a systematic review of research on Green AI, Green DL, and optimization techniques aimed at reducing the environmental impact of AI models. In addition, we examine and compare several carbon measurement tools for estimating emissions generated by AI algorithms. To complement the review, we conducted an empirical evaluation using a CPU-based experimental setup, in which six DL models were implemented for a multi-label classification task. The objective was to quantify and compare their overall carbon emissions and to determine which stages of the DL lifecycle contribute most significantly to the total footprint. The results show that the training phase is the primary source of emissions. Moreover, the findings reveal that increased architectural complexity does not systematically translate into proportional accuracy gains, highlighting the importance of carefully balancing predictive performance and environmental cost. These results reinforce the need to integrate sustainability considerations into model selection and AI system design.

</details>


### [153] [Rule of Thumb: Explaining Artificial Intelligence Systems using Partial Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10766)
*Kaivalya Rawal,Daria Onitiu,Brent Mittelstadt,Sandra Wachter,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种名为"Rule of Thumb (RoT)"的可解释AI新方法，用于解释AI系统的决策过程，适用于零样本分类、黑盒审计和科学发现。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"substantially faster than alternatives"（比替代方法快得多），但这是指XAI解释生成速度，而非模型推理速度。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。如果硬要寻找联系：XAI方法未来可能用于解释遥感目标检测模型的决策过程（例如解释为什么检测到某个舰船目标），但本文未涉及遥感场景，且与轻量化无关。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) seeks to explain how an Artificial Intelligence (AI) system arrived at a particular decision. We propose ''Rule of Thumb'' (RoT) explanations, a new approach to XAI based upon a novel formulation that identifies the most relevant features for predicting the behaviour of an AI system, for a particular datapoint. We show how RoT is well-suited to enable XAI in: (a) zero-shot classification using large language models (LLMs), (b) auditing of opaque AI systems without model access, and (c) the use of AI in scientific discovery. Additionally, RoT meets specific requirements from leading AI regulations, provides a familiar interface and visualisations for XAI practitioners, is model-agnostic, and is substantially faster than alternatives.
  Code available at: https://github.com/KaiRawal/Rule-of-Thumb-Explaining-Artificial-Intelligence-Systems-using-Partial-Information

</details>


### [154] [ReCBM: Uncertainty-Gated Relational Reasoning for Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10004)

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide an interpretable framework by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts, enabling semantic inspection and test-time intervention. Recent variants have improved CBMs through richer concept representations, uncertainty estimation, and dependency modeling. However, robust reasoning under unreliable concept states remains underexplored. Without such reasoning, misleading semantic evidence can propagate through the bottleneck, compromising both explanations and downstream predictions. To address this issue, we propose ReCBM, an uncertainty-gated relational reasoning framework for CBMs. ReCBM introduces semantically defined concept relations into the bottleneck and uses uncertainty to guide their refinement. By modeling co-occurrence, implication, and exclusion, ReCBM specifies how evidence is exchanged across concepts, while uncertainty modulates the contribution of each concept during this process. Experiments across diverse datasets showed that ReCBM improved concept and task recovery under missing and flipped concepts, supported uncertainty-aware intervention, and extracted compact task-relevant concept subsets without degrading downstream performance.

</details>

Abstract: As AI agents are increasingly deployed in complex environments, understanding their behaviors becomes critical. Yet behavioral scientific research on AI agents remains manual and labor-intensive. We introduce AEROBAT, the first multi-agent system to automate behavioral scientific research on AI agents. Given an arbitrary target behavior by its user, AEROBAT automatically executes a full pipeline of behavioral scientific research---generating hypotheses about the behavior, designing and executing controlled experiments, making behavioral assessments, analyzing the results, and writing reports. For 12 target behaviors, we used AEROBAT to generate and test 79 hypotheses: designing 1,240 controlled experiments and executing 23,512 simulation rounds in total. Moderate-to-strong statistical evidence was found for 26 hypotheses, including some novel ones. In sum, our results demonstrate that automated behavioral scientific research on AI agents can complement and extend the reach of manual research.

</details>


### [156] [CHORUS: Complementary Experts for High-Coverage Testbench Stimulus Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10090)

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced code generation, where executable feedback provides a more reliable learning signal than textual imitation alone. Hardware verification is an important application of code generation and accounts for a substantial fraction of modern chip design effort, with high-coverage testbench stimulus generation as a key task. We present CHORUS, a post-training framework that pushes performance beyond what a conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT)-to-reinforcement learning (RL) pipeline achieves. CHORUS builds on two observations. First, staged SFT produces behaviorally diverse checkpoints, and dense-reward RL turns them into strong experts with comparable aggregate performance but distinct task-level strengths. Second, these complementary strengths can be exploited through either training-free model merging or further post-training to outperform the best individual expert. By consolidating the resulting specialists into a single 4B model, CHORUS achieves 88.0% Pass@1 on CVDP-ECov, outperforming DeepSeek-R1 (671B) by 13.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [157] [MESA:Task-Adaptive Multi-Structure Evidence Selection for Long-Horizon Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10108)
*Beidi Zhao,Yaoqi Chen,Yuru Feng,Menghao Li,Qianxi Zhang,Baotong Lu,Jianan Lu,Zhirui Wang,Xinjiang Wang,Shusen Xu,Zengzhong Li,Xiaoxiao Li,Qi Chen*

Abstract: Long-horizon agents accumulate trajectories spanning hundreds of interleaved reasoning, action, and observation steps, where answering a query may depend on evidence buried far back in the history. External memory stores such trajectories as structured representations, yet each structure provides a distinct and incomplete view. Existing multi-memory systems either read a fixed set of structures for every query, inflating context and introducing noise, or route each query to a single structure, preventing the composition of complementary evidence. A controlled analysis on AMA-Bench shows that the optimal memory configuration is typically neither a single structure nor the full union, but a tailored composition of multiple structural memories that varies with query and task demands. Motivated by these findings, we formulate structure-level dynamic selection: selecting and fusing a query-adaptive subset from a library of specialized memory structures. We propose MESA (a Multi-structure Evidence Selection framework for long-horizon Agent), which builds five complementary structure views of each trajectory and learns from end-to-end answer-level feedback to select and fuse a query-specific subset for a frozen answer model. To learn under this weak supervision, MESA employs harness optimization with prior-guided search and UCB-guided scheduling to balance exploration and exploitation. On AMA-Bench, MESA outperforms the strongest baseline by 8.5% while using 41% fewer evidence tokens than the all-structure alternative.

</details>

Abstract: Enterprises are deploying autonomous AI agents faster than they can govern them, and prevailing approaches stretch a single discipline, typically DevSecOps built for deterministic automation, across every scale of agency. We argue that agentic AI governance is four problems, not one, each with a mature governing science. The CASE framework assigns Control theory to the individual agent (intent as setpoint, guardrails as feedback, evaluation as observation), complex Adaptive systems theory to agent collectives (where emergence makes single-agent assurance non-compositional), Supervisory cybernetics to human-agent teams (where the Law of Requisite Variety shows unaided human oversight fails structurally), and Engineering operations to fleets (extending error budgets to decision quality so autonomy becomes a controlled variable). We formalize each layer, derive cross-layer coupling conditions, including a zero-touch deployment paradox where excellence at one-layer strains the others, and trace twenty-plus enterprise controls to their classical constructs. Three empirical studies validate the thesis: 82 percent of documented production agent failures are multi-layer trajectories; none of 22 ecosystem tools offers full Layer 2 (emergence) coverage; and all 35 scored public deployments fall in the lowest maturity band. We name this mismatch, risk realized at the emergence layer against capability barely offered and practice absent, the Emergence Gap. A five-level maturity model with a non-compensatory bottleneck-weighted index and assessment instrument operationalizes CASE as a scientific rather than process maturity model, grounded in production enterprise agentic platforms. As EU AI Act Article 14 makes effective human oversight a legal requirement, only architectures satisfying requisite variety can make oversight real rather than ceremonial.

</details>


### [159] [SBCO: Self-Supervised, Verifier-Grounded Harness Optimization For Planning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10157)
*Vivek Kulkarni,Sudipta Paul,Aounon Kumar,Nicholas Tzou,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Self-improving agents seek to reduce the human engineering effort behind AI systems by enabling them to evolve and self-improve their performance over time. Recently, methods like the Darwin Gödel Machine and the Huxley Gödel Machine have been proposed which enable open-ended, recursive self-improvement through self-reference where a coding agent edits its own code. Such self-referential self-improvement methods require that the competence required to perform the task coincides or aligns well with the competence required for self-modification which is the case for coding tasks. For domains or tasks, which do not satisfy the alignment needed, self-referential self-improvement is not available. In such cases, it is possible to adapt the above algorithms to other tasks by removing the self-referential aspect or introducing explicit self-modification of a meta-agent -- both computationally expensive, relying on population or self-modification search over many candidate agents. For planning tasks with explicit constraints, we propose a far cheaper alternative. We introduce SBCO (Self-supervised Block Coordinate Optimizer), a verifier-grounded harness optimizer in the same closed-loop, improve-from-experience family as the Gödel-machine methods, but self-supervised rather than self-referential. Given an agentic harness, SBCO learns a decomposed bank of verifiers and a harness policy via approximate block coordinate ascent, improving the agent's outputs from its own graded feedback---with a fixed meta-agent and no human labels. Across two domains SBCO matches or exceeds a customized self-modifying baseline while using 4-5.5 times less compute budget.

</details>

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has facilitated their ubiquitous integration into various domains, leading to widespread adoption. However, this escalating trend has introduced significant security vulnerabilities, necessitating the identification and mitigation of flaws arising from malicious exploitation. Red teaming assessments, conducted to evaluate model robustness through diverse adversarial inputs, are essential for exposing security risks and implementing countermeasures. Currently, red teaming is performed either manually by experts or automatically using predefined attack datasets. Nevertheless, manual testing remains time-consuming, while existing automated methods suffer from limited creativity due to their inherent dependency on fixed datasets. In this study, we propose an automated, human-independent, and adaptive approach leveraging GFlowNets to identify LLM vulnerabilities by utilizing one large language model to test another. Within this framework, an attacker model is trained against a specified victim model to perform automated red teaming and provide a quantitative robustness score. This research aims to generate more effective adversarial attacks in English compared to existing benchmarks and, as a novel contribution to the literature, introduces a model capable of generating attack inputs in the Turkish language.

</details>


### [161] [TRACE: Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10176)

Research relevance: 弱相关（1分）。论文主题是公共服务对话系统（public service chatbot）中的约束感知推荐与检索增强生成（RAG），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或空间在轨智能。也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测或遥感场景理解。更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等模型压缩或部署技术。论文属于自然语言处理/信息检索领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Model architecture: TRACE（Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine）：基于检索的约束感知框架，将用户查询解析为结构性和语义性约束，用于下游检索；采用双数据表示模式（dual data representation schema），并对比有无知识图谱（KG）的多种知识表示变体。底层使用多种开源LLM及一个专有模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。仅提到"结果对模型大小不敏感"（less sensitive to model size），但这属于检索质量对模型规模依赖性的观察，并非轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。且该场景为公共服务对话系统，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：一个州级食品储藏室目录（statewide pantry directory）和合成查询基准（synthetic query benchmark）。评价指标：用户约束满足度（user constraint satisfaction）和幻觉推荐减少程度（reducing hallucinated recommendations）。

Experiments: 对比多种知识表示变体（有无知识图谱KG），实验多种开源LLM和一个专有模型。主要结论：强化检索能显著提升用户约束满足度并减少幻觉推荐；随着检索质量提升，不同LLM之间的性能差异缩小，结果对模型大小敏感性降低。

Limitations: 摘要未说明。但可推断：仅针对公共服务目录这一特定领域，未涉及遥感/视觉任务；未讨论模型压缩或部署；未提供计算资源、推理速度等工程指标；合成查询基准可能缺乏真实用户多样性。

Abstract: Public service chatbots are expected to deliver recommendations from an underlying public service directory, while also making sure that the recommendations respect explicit user constraints. In practice, public service directories are noisy and inconsistent, and general-purpose large language model (LLM) or AI-based chatbots frequently generate unreliable recommendations, citing unverified sources from the web. We investigate the impact of retrieval quality on constraint-aware recommendation in public service conversational systems built over noisy and heterogeneous service directories. We propose TRACE (Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine), a retrieval-based, constraint-aware framework that parses input user queries into structural and semantic constraints for downstream retrieval, with the help of a dual data representation schema. Using a curated statewide pantry directory and a synthetic query benchmark, we evaluate multiple knowledge-representation variants with and without knowledge graphs (KGs). We experiment with several open-source LLMs and a proprietary model, showing that strengthening retrieval substantially improves user constraint satisfaction while reducing hallucinated recommendations. Performance differences across LLMs narrowed in our experiments as retrieval quality improved, making results less sensitive to model size. These findings suggest that the quality of retrieval is key for robust public service conversational systems.

</details>


### [162] [Post-Hoc Sparse Coding of Latent Communication Between Vision-Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10198)
*Di Wu,Xiaohui Zhu*

Abstract: Latent-space communication allows heterogeneous vision-language model agents to exchange continuous representations without serializing visual and reasoning states into text. Vision Wormhole realizes this approach by translating visual features into a universal latent representation that can be consumed by another model, but every message is transported as a dense tensor of the same size regardless of its content. A fixed-capacity dense tensor therefore need not have a fixed effective information density: some messages may use only a small fraction of the available representational degrees of freedom. This observation suggests that the communication channel may be substantially compressible. We study its redundancy by fitting a post-hoc sparse autoencoder to frozen Vision Wormhole activations and measuring reconstruction, downstream utility, feature reuse, and token-level interventions across nine reasoning benchmarks. Relative to the original float32 transport, a uint16-index/float16-value sparse payload with k=4 active coefficients per token reduces the transmitted bytes by 128x. In a single-run evaluation, the seven-task non-AIME mean accuracy changes from 49.85% to 49.77%. The fitted 4096-element dictionary uses only 50 features, and task-level active sets have a mean pairwise Jaccard similarity of 0.906. These measurements establish strong post-hoc compressibility relative to the original transport, but do not yet isolate the incremental contribution of sparse coding from position selection, reduced precision, low-rank structure, or SAE optimization effects. The results motivate matched-payload comparisons and communication mechanisms whose payload adapts to the information used by each message.

</details>


### [163] [Edge Phoneme Recognition for Children's Speech through Age-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10206)
*Matthew Arboleda,Ryan Arboleda,Sophie Haak,Sam Hjelmeset,Andrew Franck,Bingrui Yang,Jose Bustamante Ortiz,Yuanrong Shen,Joel Walsh*

Experiments: 94M参数模型在DrivenData目标分布上超越WavLM Large（317M），并接近90倍参数量的竞赛集成模型（差距约0.04 CER）。未报告消融实验、速度、参数量之外的详细对比。

Limitations: 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关，属于语音领域。轻量化仅体现在"小模型+辅助任务"，未涉及通用模型压缩技术。缺乏FLOPs、延迟、能耗等部署量化指标。辅助任务（年龄预测）的通用性有限，难以直接迁移到遥感目标检测。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴的通用思路：通过多任务辅助学习（auxiliary task）提升小模型在目标分布上的表现，即在不增加推理成本的前提下，用辅助监督信号增强轻量模型的表征能力。这一思想可迁移到遥感在轨目标检测——例如在轻量检测头之外增加"场景分类/地物分割/传感器参数回归"等辅助任务，帮助小模型在星上有限算力下保持检测精度。但需注意：该论文未提供剪枝/量化/蒸馏等具体压缩手段，且场景差异大，借鉴价值主要在于"多任务辅助提升轻量模型"这一方法论层面。

Abstract: Detecting phonemes from children's speech has historically been difficult due to the scarcity of training data, and unique characteristics of children's speech. During a phoneme detection competition, we found that training a lightweight model to predict the age of the learner, as well as the phoneme sequence, enabled a 94M-parameter model to outperform WavLM Large models (317M) on the target DrivenData distribution, and fall within approximately 0.04 CER of competition ensembles with 90 times the parameters. This has enabled the creation of PhonemeTrainer, an application that can run on most modern cellular phones. This will ultimately enable better Automated Speech Recognition (ASR) and pronunciation helper apps for children's speech, with the privacy and compliance benefits that come with edge processing.

</details>

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是地面公交系统的串车控制问题，属于城市交通管理领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关。不涉及任何目标检测任务，也不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等方向。仅LLM辅助嵌入生成的方法论在广义上可视为一种特征表示学习，但与本研究方向无直接关联。

Task and scene: 任务类型：公交串车控制（bus bunching control），基于强化学习的holding控制。应用场景：城市高频公交系统。传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器。目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明任何遥感相关任务。

Experiments: 与Daganzo基线方法对比，语义控制器将车头时距变异性降低32.0%，串车事件减少69.2%，乘客等待时间减少24.0%。跨路线实验表明零样本迁移泛化有限，warm-start微调可加速早期学习，但冷启动训练最终性能最佳。
